Please write a comprehensive primer of ancient Egyptian pyramids for an artist with no technical background, covering how it fails and what happens when it does, the key components and their roles, the role of scale, and where the analogy breaks down. Do not assume prior knowledge beyond high school math. Use an analogy, then explain its limits. Include at least two concrete examples. Take your time and cover each part fully before moving on.
